function commandName(): string {
    const value = String(process.argv[3] || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (['help', '-h', '--help'].includes(value)) return 'help';
    if (!value || value.startsWith('--')) return 'start';
    return value;
}

function printRemoteUsage(): void {
    console.log([
        'Usage: mcp-device [command]',
        '',
        'Commands: start, login, logout, status, install, stop, uninstall'
    ].join('\n'));
}

async function persistGatewayPayload(
    store: GatewayDeviceStatusStore,
    gatewayUrl: string,
    identity: GatewayDeviceIdentity,
    payload: any
): Promise<GatewayDeviceStatus> {
    const record = await identity.loadOrCreate();
    const current = await store.load();
    return await store.update({
        gatewayUrl,
        deviceId: record.deviceId,
        deviceName: payload?.device?.name || current.deviceName || os.hostname(),
        identityPresent: true,
        account: payload?.account || current.account,
        connection: {
            online: payload?.device?.online !== false,
            connectionEpoch: payload?.device?.connectionEpoch ?? current.connection.connectionEpoch,
            lastConnectedAt: payload?.device?.connectedAt || Date.now()
        },
        usage: payload?.usage ?? current.usage,
        schema: payload?.schema ?? current.schema,
        usageFreshAt: Date.now()
    });
}

function controlAdapter(): GatewayToolAdapter {
    return { async call() { throw new Error('Tool calls are disabled for this control-only connection.'); } } as unknown as GatewayToolAdapter;
}

async function lifecycleSnapshot(status: GatewayDeviceStatus) {
    const owner = await probeRuntimeOwner();
    if (process.platform === 'win32' && status.deviceId) {
        const service = await new WindowsDeviceService().status(status.deviceId);
        const managers = service.installed ? [{
            manager: service.registration === 'task' ? 'windows-task' : service.registration === 'run' ? 'windows-run' : 'windows-manual',
            installed: service.installed,
            running: service.running,
            autostart: service.autostart
        }] : [];
        return { owner, service, managers };
    }
    if (process.platform === 'linux') {
        const managers = await discoverLinuxManagers();
        const installed = managers.some(manager => manager.installed);
        const running = managers.some(manager => manager.running);
        const autostart = managers.some(manager => manager.autostart);
        return { owner, service: { installed, running, autostart }, managers };
    }
    return { owner, service: { installed: false, running: false, autostart: false }, managers: [] };
}

async function confirmRuntimeTakeover(existing: RuntimeOwnerStatus): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!process.stdin.isTTY || !process.stdout.isTTY) return false;
    const rl = readline.createInterface({ input: process.stdin, output: process.stdout });
    try {
        const answer = await rl.question(`MCP Device is running in ${existing.mode} mode (PID ${existing.pid}). Stop it and take over? [Y/n] `);
        return !['n', 'no'].includes(String(answer || '').trim().toLowerCase());
    } finally {
        rl.close();
    }
}

export async function applyServiceCommandStatus(
    store: GatewayDeviceStatusStore,
    command: 'install' | 'start' | 'stop' | 'uninstall'
): Promise<void> {
    if (command === 'stop' || command === 'uninstall') await store.markOffline();
}

async function showStatus(json: boolean): Promise<void> {
    const store = new GatewayDeviceStatusStore();
    const status = await store.load();
    const config = await new GatewayDeviceConfigStore().load();
    const lifecycle = await lifecycleSnapshot(status);
    const effectiveStatus = effectiveGatewayStatus(status, { runtimeOwned: Boolean(lifecycle.owner), serviceRunning: lifecycle.service.running });
    console.log(formatGatewayStatus(effectiveStatus, {
        json,
        service: lifecycle.service,
        runtime: lifecycle.owner ? { mode: lifecycle.owner.mode, pid: lifecycle.owner.pid } : null,
        managers: lifecycle.managers,
        security: { protocolFloor: config.securityProtocolFloor, appCaProvisioned: Boolean(config.appCaPem) },
        proxy: { mode: config.proxy.mode }
    }));
}

async function withBootstrapOwner<T>(action: () => Promise<T>, allowTakeover = true): Promise<T> {
    const owner = new RuntimeOwner({ mode: 'bootstrap' });
    await acquireRuntimeOwner(owner, { confirmTakeover: allowTakeover ? confirmRuntimeTakeover : undefined });
    try {
        await owner.bootstrap();
        await bootstrapOfficialGatewayTrust(new GatewayDeviceConfigStore());
        return await action();
    } finally {
        await owner.release();
    }
}

export async function resolvePairingIdentity(
    identity: GatewayDeviceIdentity,
    options: { forcePair?: boolean; probeCurrentIdentity?: () => Promise<void> } = {}
) {
    const current = await identity.loadOrCreate();
    if (!options.forcePair || deviceIdentityNeedsPairing(current)) return current;
    if (typeof options.probeCurrentIdentity !== 'function') {
        throw new Error('Current device identity probe is required before forced pairing.');
    }
    try {
        await options.probeCurrentIdentity();
        return current;
    } catch (error: any) {
        if (String(error?.code || '') !== 'DEVICE_FORGOTTEN') throw error;
        await identity.forget();
        return await identity.loadOrCreate();
    }
}

async function ensureLinkedDeviceUnderOwner(forcePair = false): Promise<{ deviceId: string; accountLabel: string | null }> {
    const store = new GatewayDeviceStatusStore();
    const current = await store.load();
    const config = await captureGatewayConfigFromEnvironment(new GatewayDeviceConfigStore(), { fallbackGatewayUrl: current.gatewayUrl });
    const gatewayUrl = String(config.gatewayUrl || '').trim();
    const identity = new GatewayDeviceIdentity();
    let record = await identity.loadOrCreate();
    const initialDeviceId = record.deviceId;
    await store.update({
        gatewayUrl,
        deviceId: record.deviceId,
        deviceName: current.deviceName || os.hostname(),
        identityPresent: true
    });
    if (!forcePair && !deviceIdentityNeedsPairing(record)) {
        return { deviceId: record.deviceId, accountLabel: current.account.label };
    }

    const proxy = createGatewayProxyAgent(config);
    let channel: GatewayDeviceChannel | undefined;
    try {
        record = await resolvePairingIdentity(identity, {
            forcePair,
            probeCurrentIdentity: async () => {
                const probe = new GatewayDeviceChannel({
                    gatewayUrl,
                    identity,
                    proxyAgent: proxy.agent,
                    securityProtocolFloor: config.securityProtocolFloor,
                    appCaPem: config.appCaPem || undefined,
                    adapter: controlAdapter(),
                    agentVersion: process.env.npm_package_version
                });
                try { await probe.start(); }
                finally { await probe.stop().catch(() => {}); }
            }
        });
        if (record.deviceId !== initialDeviceId) {
            await store.update({
                gatewayUrl,
                deviceId: record.deviceId,
                deviceName: os.hostname(),
                identityPresent: true,
                account: { connected: false, label: null },
                connection: { ...current.connection, online: false }
            });
        }
        const pairing = await pairGatewayDevice({ gatewayUrl, identity, proxyAgent: proxy.agent, deviceName: os.hostname() });
        await store.update({
            gatewayUrl,
            deviceId: record.deviceId,
            deviceName: os.hostname(),
            identityPresent: true,
            account: { connected: false, label: pairing.account.label },
            connection: { ...current.connection, online: false }
        });
        channel = new GatewayDeviceChannel({
            gatewayUrl,
            pairingGrant: pairing.enrollmentGrant,
            identity,
            proxyAgent: proxy.agent,
            securityProtocolFloor: config.securityProtocolFloor,
            appCaPem: config.appCaPem || undefined,
            adapter: controlAdapter(),
            agentVersion: process.env.npm_package_version,
            onStatus: async payload => { await persistGatewayPayload(store, gatewayUrl, identity, payload); }
        });
        await channel.start();
        const linked = await store.load();
        return { deviceId: record.deviceId, accountLabel: linked.account.label };
    } finally {
        await channel?.stop().catch(() => {});
        proxy.agent.destroy();
        await store.markOffline().catch(() => {});
    }
}

async function login(): Promise<void> {
    return await withBootstrapOwner(async () => {
        const linked = await ensureLinkedDeviceUnderOwner(true);
        console.log(`Account connected: ${linked.accountLabel || 'gateway account'}`);
    });
}

async function logout(): Promise<void> {
    return await withBootstrapOwner(async () => {
    const store = new GatewayDeviceStatusStore();
    const current = await store.load();
    const config = await new GatewayDeviceConfigStore().load();
    const gatewayUrl = String(config.gatewayUrl || current.gatewayUrl || '').trim();
    if (!gatewayUrl || !current.identityPresent) throw new Error('No connected gateway identity is available to log out.');
    const proxy = createGatewayProxyAgent(config);
    const identity = new GatewayDeviceIdentity();
    const channel = new GatewayDeviceChannel({
        gatewayUrl,
        identity,
        proxyAgent: proxy.agent,
        securityProtocolFloor: config.securityProtocolFloor,
        appCaPem: config.appCaPem || undefined,
        adapter: controlAdapter(),
        agentVersion: process.env.npm_package_version,
        onStatus: async payload => { await persistGatewayPayload(store, gatewayUrl, identity, payload); }
    });
    try {
        await channel.start();
        await channel.logoutAccount();
        console.log('Account disconnected from this device.');
    } finally {
        await channel.stop().catch(() => {});
        proxy.agent.destroy();
        await store.markOffline().catch(() => {});
    }
    });
}

async function waitForOwnerRelease(timeoutMs = 5000): Promise<void> {
    const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        if (!await probeRuntimeOwner()) return;
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
    }
    throw new Error('MCP Device runtime did not release ownership after authenticated stop.');
}

async function serviceCommand(command: 'install' | 'stop' | 'uninstall'): Promise<void> {
    const store = new GatewayDeviceStatusStore();
    const owner = await probeRuntimeOwner();
    if ((command === 'stop' || command === 'uninstall') && owner) {
        if (!await stopRuntimeOwner()) throw new Error('MCP Device runtime did not accept authenticated stop.');
        await waitForOwnerRelease();
        await store.markOffline();
        if (command === 'stop') {
            console.log('MCP Device stop requested.');
            return;
        }
    }

    if (!['win32', 'linux'].includes(process.platform)) {
        throw new Error('Background lifecycle is currently supported on Windows and Linux only.');
    }

    if (command === 'install') {
        const linuxManager = process.platform === 'linux' ? await chooseLinuxManager() : null;
        const deviceId = await withBootstrapOwner(async () => {
            const linked = await ensureLinkedDeviceUnderOwner(false);
            if (process.platform === 'win32') await new WindowsDeviceService().install({ deviceId: linked.deviceId });
            else if (linuxManager === 'pm2') await new Pm2DeviceService().install();
            else await new SystemdUserDeviceService().install();
            return linked.deviceId;
        });
        if (process.platform === 'win32') await new WindowsDeviceService().start(deviceId);
        console.log(`Background device installed and started: ${deviceId}`);
        return;
    }

    if (command === 'stop') {
        const status = await store.load();
        if (process.platform === 'win32') {
            if (!status.deviceId || !status.identityPresent) throw new Error('No installed MCP Device identity was found.');
            await new WindowsDeviceService().stop(status.deviceId);
        } else {
            const systemd = new SystemdUserDeviceService();
            const pm2 = new Pm2DeviceService();
            if ((await systemd.status()).installed) await systemd.stop();
            if ((await pm2.status()).installed) await pm2.stop();
        }
        await applyServiceCommandStatus(store, command);
        console.log('MCP Device background runtime stop requested.');
        return;
    }

    await withBootstrapOwner(async () => {
        const status = await store.load();
        if (process.platform === 'win32') {
            if (!status.deviceId || !status.identityPresent) throw new Error('No installed MCP Device identity was found.');
            await new WindowsDeviceService().uninstall(status.deviceId);
        } else {
            await new SystemdUserDeviceService().uninstall();
            await new Pm2DeviceService().uninstall();
        }
        await applyServiceCommandStatus(store, command);
    }, false);
    console.log('MCP Device background registration removed; identity and account state were preserved.');
}

export async function runRemote() {
    // MCP Device owns its account-scoped usage telemetry at the gateway.

    const disableNoSleep = process.argv.includes('--disable-no-sleep');
    const verbose = process.argv.includes('--debug');
    if (!verbose) console.debug = () => { };
    else console.debug('[DEBUG] Verbose mode:', verbose);

    const command = commandName();
    if (command === 'help') return printRemoteUsage();
    if (command === 'status') return await showStatus(process.argv.includes('--json'));
    if (command === 'login') return await login();
    if (command === 'logout') return await logout();
    if (command === 'install' || command === 'stop' || command === 'uninstall') {
        return await serviceCommand(command);
    }
    if (command !== 'start') {
        throw new Error('Unknown MCP Device command. Use start, status, login, logout, install, stop, or uninstall.');
    }

    if (!disableNoSleep && os.platform() === 'darwin') {
        try {
            const { default: caffeinate } = await import('caffeinate');
            caffeinate({ pid: process.pid });
            console.log('No sleep mode enabled');
        } catch (error) {
            console.warn('Failed to start caffeinate:', error);
        }
    }

    const device = new MCPDevice();
    await device.start({ confirmTakeover: process.argv.includes('--service') ? undefined : confirmRuntimeTakeover });
}

===== BEGIN SOURCE src/platform/activity-journal.ts | original-bytes=3688 | sha256=74894f0fa898d2c2cd1488955a5ac22d6a990b343a20ac0054895249a06010dd =====
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';

/** Bounded platform observations. A receipt describes execution, never validation of a claim. */
export class ActivityJournal {
  private tail: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
  private pending = 0;
  private closed = false;
  private filename: string;
  private segmentTime = 0;
  private readonly directory: string;
  constructor(stateRoot: string, private deviceId: string, private runtimeId: string, private maxBytes = 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
    this.directory = path.join(stateRoot, 'logs');
    this.filename = this.nextSegment();
  }
  private nextSegment(): string {
    this.segmentTime = Math.max(Date.now(), this.segmentTime + 1);
    return path.join(this.directory, `device-activity-${this.segmentTime}-${randomUUID()}.jsonl`);
  }
  record(tool: string, args: unknown, result?: any, error?: unknown): void {
    if (this.closed || this.pending >= 256 || tool.startsWith('knowledge.')) return;
    const input = args && typeof args === 'object' ? args as Record<string, unknown> : {};
    const paths: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const name of ['path', 'file_path', 'working_directory', 'cwd']) if (typeof input[name] === 'string') paths[name] = (input[name] as string).slice(0, 1024);
    let value = result?.structuredContent ?? result;
    const text = value?.content?.find?.((part: any) => part.type === 'text')?.text;
    if (typeof text === 'string' && text.length < 64 * 1024) { try { value = JSON.parse(text); } catch { /* no raw output is persisted */ } }
    const exitCode = typeof value?.exitCode === 'number' ? value.exitCode : typeof value?.exit_code === 'number' ? value.exit_code : null;
    const failed = Boolean(error || result?.isError || value?.isError || value?.success === false || value?.timedOut || (exitCode !== null && exitCode !== 0));
    const running = !failed && exitCode === null && Boolean(value?.sessionId || value?.session_id);
    const status = failed ? 'failed' : running ? 'running' : 'completed';
    const receipt = {
      version: 1, event_id: randomUUID(), timestamp: new Date().toISOString(), device_id: this.deviceId,
      session_id: this.runtimeId, toolName: tool.slice(0, 96), arguments: paths,
      output: `Device tool ${tool.slice(0, 96)} ${status}${exitCode === null ? '' : ' with exit code ' + exitCode}.`,
      status, exit_code: exitCode,
      ...(error ? { error: { code: String((error as { code?: unknown }).code ?? 'TOOL_CALL_FAILED').slice(0, 96) } } : {})
    };
    const frame = JSON.stringify(receipt) + '\n';
    this.pending++;
    this.tail = this.tail.then(async () => {
      await fs.mkdir(this.directory, { recursive: true });
      let size = await fs.stat(this.filename).then(stat => stat.size, () => 0);
      if (size > 0 && size + Buffer.byteLength(frame) > this.maxBytes) { this.filename = this.nextSegment(); size = 0; }
      await fs.appendFile(this.filename, frame, { mode: 0o600 });
      if (size === 0) {
        // Stable paths keep exact-source references valid until intentional
        // retention pruning. Every segment remains discoverable as JSONL.
        const segments = (await fs.readdir(this.directory)).filter(name => /^device-activity-\d+-[0-9a-f-]+\.jsonl$/.test(name) && name !== path.basename(this.filename)).sort().reverse();
        for (const name of segments.slice(3)) await fs.rm(path.join(this.directory, name), { force: true });
      }
    }).catch(() => { /* Observation failure never changes the underlying tool result. */ }).finally(() => { this.pending--; });
  }
  async close(): Promise<void> { this.closed = true; await this.tail; }
}

const packageRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../', import.meta.url));
const usage = `MCP Device 1.0.12
  mcp-device start                         Existing Device-only runtime
  mcp-device device [start|status|...]      Alias for the Device-only commands
  mcp-device server [--runtime-dir DIR] [--host HOST] [--port PORT] [--loopback-port PORT] [--public-url URL] [--skill-root DIR] [--allow-http-lan] [--legacy-knowledge-mcp]
  mcp-device admin [--runtime-dir DIR] account|invite ...
  mcp-device plugins [--runtime-dir DIR] list|install|enable|disable|uninstall|purge|upgrade|configure ...
  mcp-device plugins install palace --bundled --enable  Reinstall bundled Palace after an uninstall
  mcp-device plugins upgrade palace --bundled  Upgrade the bundled Palace contract safely
  mcp-device plugins repair-lock              Recover a proved-abandoned/incomplete registry lock
  mcp-device knowledge [--runtime-dir DIR] search|list|read|ingest|learn|correct|skills [JSON]
  mcp-device state backup|verify|restore|migrate [--source DIR] [--backup DIR] [--target DIR]
  mcp-device context ...                   Existing local Context commands
  mcp-device logs ...                      Existing P1 log search commands

Server state defaults to ~/.mcp-device/server. Device state defaults stay unchanged.
Server clients, including localhost, use the ordinary account/OAuth and Device pairing flow.
Install/upgrade takes a local plugin manifest JSON. Disable/uninstall retain plugin data.
Purge is a separate explicit operation for a disabled plugin. Admin passwords come from HCU_ADMIN_PASSWORD.
State migration defaults to a reviewable dry run; --apply writes only a new target after verified backups.
`;
interface Parsed { values: Record<string, string[]>; flags: Set<string>; positional: string[]; }
function parse(argv: string[]): Parsed {
  const values: Record<string, string[]> = {}, flags = new Set<string>(), positional: string[] = [];
  const valued = new Set(['runtime-dir', 'host', 'port', 'loopback-port', 'public-url', 'skill-root', 'project-root', 'source-root', 'config', 'source', 'backup', 'target', 'device-root', 'gateway-root']);
  const switches = new Set(['help', 'enable', 'bundled', 'apply', 'json', 'allow-http-lan', 'legacy-knowledge-mcp']);
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const arg = argv[i];
    if (arg === '--') { positional.push(...argv.slice(i + 1)); break; }
    if (arg === '-h') { flags.add('help'); continue; }
    if (!arg.startsWith('--')) { positional.push(arg); continue; }
    const equal = arg.indexOf('='), key = arg.slice(2, equal < 0 ? undefined : equal);
    if (switches.has(key) && equal < 0) { flags.add(key); continue; }
    if (!valued.has(key)) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Unknown option: --' + key);
    const value = equal < 0 ? argv[++i] : arg.slice(equal + 1);
    if (!value || value.startsWith('--')) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Missing value for --' + key);
    (values[key] ??= []).push(value);
  }
  return { values, flags, positional };
}
const one = (args: Parsed, key: string, fallback?: string): string | undefined => args.values[key]?.[args.values[key].length - 1] ?? fallback;
function stateRoot(args: Parsed, serverDefault = false): string {
  return path.resolve(one(args, 'runtime-dir', process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR || path.join(os.homedir(), '.mcp-device', ...(serverDefault ? ['server'] : [])))!);
}
function required(args: Parsed, key: string): string {
  const value = one(args, key); if (!value) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', '--' + key + ' is required'); return path.resolve(value);
}
async function jsonFile(filename: string): Promise<any> {
  const text = await fs.readFile(path.resolve(filename), 'utf8');
  return JSON.parse(text.replace(/^\uFEFF/, ''));
}
const print = (value: unknown) => process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + '\n');
const event = (value: unknown) => process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(value) + '\n');

async function pluginCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  const { PluginHost } = await import('./plugin-host.js');
  const root = await resolveRuntimeRoot(one(args, 'runtime-dir')), [action = 'list', target, extra] = args.positional;
  const host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'local-plugin-management' });
  let release: (() => Promise<void>) | undefined;
  await host.load();
  try {
    if (action === 'list') { print({ runtime_dir: root, plugins: host.list(), diagnostics: host.diagnostics() }); return; }
    if (action === 'repair-lock') { print({runtime_dir:root,...await host.repairRegistryLock()}); return; }
    if (!target) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Plugin id or manifest file is required');
    if (action === 'install' || action === 'upgrade') {
      let manifest: PluginManifest;
      if(args.flags.has('bundled')){
        const { bundledPluginManifests }=await import('../distribution.js');
        const bundled=bundledPluginManifests().find(item=>item.id===target);
        if(!bundled)throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND','Unknown bundled plugin: '+target);
        manifest=bundled;
      }else{
        manifest=await jsonFile(target) as PluginManifest;
        manifest.entry=path.resolve(path.dirname(path.resolve(target)),manifest.entry);
      }
      if (action === 'install') await host.install(manifest, { enabled: args.flags.has('enable'), installationSource: args.flags.has('bundled')?'distribution':'manual' });
      else {
        // Migration must not race a running provider in a different process.
        const { acquireServerLease } = await import('./runtime-lease.js');
        release = await acquireServerLease(root);
        await host.upgrade(manifest,{installationSource:args.flags.has('bundled')?'distribution':'manual'});
      }
    } else if (action === 'configure') {
      if (!extra) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'configure requires a settings JSON file');
      const settings = await jsonFile(extra);
      if (!settings || Array.isArray(settings) || typeof settings !== 'object') throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Plugin settings must be an object');
      const record = host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === target);
      if (record?.enabled) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_STILL_ENABLED', 'Disable the plugin before changing its settings');
      await host.configure(target, settings);
    } else if (['enable', 'disable', 'uninstall'].includes(action)) {
      await host[action as 'enable' | 'disable' | 'uninstall'](target);
    } else if (action === 'purge') {
      const { acquireServerLease } = await import('./runtime-lease.js');
      release = await acquireServerLease(root); await host.purge(target);
    } else throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Unknown plugins action: ' + action);
  } finally { await host.close(); await release?.(); }
  print({ ok: true, action, plugins: host.list() });
}

async function knowledgeCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  const root = await resolveRuntimeRoot(one(args, 'runtime-dir')), [action = 'search', payload = '{}'] = args.positional;
  if (!['search', 'list', 'read', 'ingest', 'learn', 'correct', 'skills'].includes(action)) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Unknown knowledge action');
  const input = payload.startsWith('@') ? await jsonFile(payload.slice(1)) : JSON.parse(payload);
  const { acquireServerLease } = await import('./runtime-lease.js');
  const release = await acquireServerLease(root);
  let host: import('./plugin-host.js').PluginHost | undefined;
  try {
    const { GatewayDeviceIdentity } = await import('../device/gateway-identity.js');
    const { PluginHost } = await import('./plugin-host.js');
    const { bundledPluginManifests } = await import('../distribution.js');
    const record = await new GatewayDeviceIdentity(path.join(root, 'gateway-identity.json')).loadOrCreate();
    host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: record.deviceId, projectRoots: args.values['project-root']?.map(item => path.resolve(item)), sourceRoots: args.values['source-root']?.map(item => path.resolve(item)) ?? [path.join(root, 'logs')] });
    await host.ensureBundled(bundledPluginManifests()); await host.start(); print(await host.invoke('knowledge.' + action, input));
  } finally { await host?.close(); await release(); }
}

async function adminCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  const root = stateRoot(args, true);
  await fs.mkdir(root, { recursive: true });
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [path.join(packageRoot, 'control-plane', 'scripts', 'admin.mjs'), ...args.positional], {
    stdio: 'inherit', windowsHide: true, env: { ...process.env, MCP_RUNTIME_DIR: root, MCP_GATEWAY_DB_PATH: path.join(root, 'gateway.sqlite') }
  });
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => { child.once('error', reject); child.once('exit', code => { process.exitCode = code ?? 1; resolve(); }); });
}

async function serverCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  if (args.positional.length && !(args.positional.length === 1 && args.positional[0] === 'start')) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'server accepts only start; use Ctrl-C or the service manager to stop');
  const root = stateRoot(args, true), port = Number(one(args, 'port', '8104'));
  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Invalid server port');
  const localPortInput = one(args, 'loopback-port');
  const loopbackPort = localPortInput === undefined ? undefined : Number(localPortInput);
  if (loopbackPort !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(loopbackPort) || loopbackPort < 1 || loopbackPort > 65535 || loopbackPort === port))
    throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Loopback MCP port must be a distinct port in the range 1..65535');
  const { acquireServerLease } = await import('./runtime-lease.js');
  const release = await acquireServerLease(root);
  process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR = root;
  let core: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof import('./device-runtime.js').createPlatformDeviceCore>> | undefined;
  let runtime: any;
  try {
    const config = one(args, 'config') ? await jsonFile(one(args, 'config')!) : {};
    if (!config || typeof config !== 'object' || Array.isArray(config) || (config.env && (typeof config.env !== 'object' || Array.isArray(config.env)))) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Server config must be a JSON object with an optional env object');
    const publicUrl = one(args, 'public-url');
    const allowHttpLan = args.flags.has('allow-http-lan');
    if (allowHttpLan) {
      if (!publicUrl) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', '--allow-http-lan requires --public-url http://<lan-host>:<port>');
      let url: URL;
      try { url = new URL(publicUrl); } catch { throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', '--public-url must be a valid absolute URL'); }
      if (url.protocol !== 'http:') throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', '--allow-http-lan is only for an explicit http:// LAN public URL');
    }
    const configuredSkillRoot = one(args, 'skill-root', process.env.MCP_SKILL_ROOT);
    const legacySharedSkillRoot = path.join(os.homedir(), '.mcp-device', 'skills');
    const skillRoot = configuredSkillRoot
      ? path.resolve(configuredSkillRoot)
      : await fs.stat(legacySharedSkillRoot).then(stat => stat.isDirectory() ? legacySharedSkillRoot : undefined).catch(() => undefined);
    const { GatewayDeviceIdentity } = await import('../device/gateway-identity.js');
    const identity = new GatewayDeviceIdentity(path.join(root, 'gateway-identity.json'));
    const record = await identity.loadOrCreate();
    const { createPlatformDeviceCore } = await import('./device-runtime.js');
    const legacyKnowledgeMcp = args.flags.has('legacy-knowledge-mcp');
    core = await createPlatformDeviceCore({ deviceId: record.deviceId, stateRoot: root, projectRoots: args.values['project-root']?.map(item => path.resolve(item)), sourceRoots: args.values['source-root']?.map(item => path.resolve(item)), exposeKnowledgeMcp: legacyKnowledgeMcp });
    const { createPlatformTools } = await import('./knowledge-mcp.js');
    const { startServerRuntime } = await import(pathToFileURL(path.join(packageRoot, 'control-plane', 'scripts', 'authenticated-mcp-wrapper.mjs')).href);
    const configPath = typeof config.configPath === 'string' ? path.resolve(path.dirname(path.resolve(one(args, 'config')!)), config.configPath) : undefined;
    runtime = await startServerRuntime({ core, identity, stateRoot: root, packageRoot, port, loopbackPort, host: one(args, 'host', '127.0.0.1'), publicUrl, allowHttpLan, skillRoot, env: config.env ?? {}, configPath, extraTools: legacyKnowledgeMcp ? await createPlatformTools(root) : [],
      deviceName: 'localhost',
      onPairing: (pairing: unknown) => event({ event: 'device_pairing_required', pairing }) });
    event({ event: 'server_ready', version: '1.0.12', url: runtime.baseUrl, ...(runtime.loopbackUrl ? { loopback_url: runtime.loopbackUrl } : {}), device_id: record.deviceId, runtime_dir: root, local_runtime: 'shared-device-core', authentication: 'account-oauth-and-pairing' });
    await new Promise<void>(resolve => {
      // A process manager may use Node's private parent/child IPC channel to stop
      // gracefully on Windows, where kill(SIGTERM) otherwise terminates abruptly.
      const message = (value: unknown) => { if ((value as { type?: string })?.type === 'mcp-device:shutdown') stop(); };
      const stop = () => { process.removeListener('SIGINT', stop); process.removeListener('SIGTERM', stop); process.removeListener('message', message); process.removeListener('disconnect', stop); resolve(); };
      process.once('SIGINT', stop); process.once('SIGTERM', stop);
      if (process.send) { process.on('message', message); process.once('disconnect', stop); }
      runtime.server.once('close', stop);
    });
  } finally { if (runtime) await runtime.close(); else await core?.close(); await release(); if (process.connected) process.disconnect(); }
}

async function stateCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  // The implementation is imported only for explicit state-management commands.
  const modulePath = new URL('./state-backup.js', import.meta.url).href;
  const state = await import(modulePath);
  const [action] = args.positional;
  if (action === 'backup') print(await state.createStateBackup({ sourceRoot: required(args, 'source'), backupRoot: required(args, 'backup') }));
  else if (action === 'verify') print(await state.verifyStateBackup({ backupRoot: required(args, 'backup') }));
  else if (action === 'restore') print(await state.restoreStateBackup({ backupRoot: required(args, 'backup'), targetRoot: required(args, 'target') }));
  else if (action === 'migrate') {
    const options = { deviceRoot: required(args, 'device-root'), gatewayRoot: required(args, 'gateway-root'), targetRoot: required(args, 'target'), backupRoot: required(args, 'backup') };
    const plan = await state.planLegacyMigration(options);
    print(args.flags.has('apply') ? await state.applyLegacyMigration({ plan }) : plan);
  } else throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Use state backup, verify, restore or migrate');
}

export async function runPlatformCli(command: string, argv: string[]): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const args = parse(argv);
    if (command === 'help' || args.flags.has('help')) { process.stdout.write(usage); return; }
    if (command === 'server') await serverCli(args);
    else if (command === 'plugins') await pluginCli(args);
    else if (command === 'knowledge') await knowledgeCli(args);
    else if (command === 'admin') await adminCli(args);
    else if (command === 'state') await stateCli(args);
    else throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Unknown platform command');
  } catch (error) { process.stderr.write(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, code: (error as CapabilityError).code ?? 'COMMAND_FAILED', error: (error as Error).message }) + '\n'); process.exitCode = 1; }
}

===== BEGIN SOURCE src/platform/device-runtime.ts | original-bytes=4986 | sha256=6c9a023e602aac0afdd62a45e4dd2b61092321bbd036a017ac6afc9ccd8719ac =====
import path from 'node:path';
import { createDeviceCore, type DeviceCore, type DeviceCoreOptions } from '../device/core.js';
import { bundledPluginManifests } from '../distribution.js';
import { PluginHost } from './plugin-host.js';
import { ActivityJournal } from './activity-journal.js';
import { compatible, CapabilityError, type Capability } from './plugin-contract.js';

export interface PlatformDeviceCore extends DeviceCore {
  readonly plugins: PluginHost;
  readonly capabilityContracts: Capability[];
  onCapabilitiesChanged(listener: () => void): () => void;
}
export async function createPlatformDeviceCore(options: DeviceCoreOptions & { projectRoots?: string[]; sourceRoots?: string[]; bundledPlugins?: boolean; exposeKnowledgeMcp?: boolean }): Promise<PlatformDeviceCore> {
  const core = await createDeviceCore(options);
  const activity = new ActivityJournal(core.stateRoot, core.deviceId, core.runtimeInstanceId);
  const plugins = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: core.stateRoot, deviceId: core.deviceId, projectRoots: options.projectRoots ?? options.allowedRoots ?? [], sourceRoots: options.sourceRoots ?? [path.join(core.stateRoot, 'logs')] });
  try { if (options.bundledPlugins !== false) await plugins.ensureBundled(bundledPluginManifests()); await plugins.start(); }
  catch (error) { process.stderr.write(`[plugins] Native Device Core remains available: ${(error as Error).message}\n`); }
  let closed = false;
  let closeRequest: Promise<void> | undefined;
  const pluginContracts = () => plugins.list().filter(record => record.installed && record.enabled && record.state === 'enabled').flatMap(record => record.manifest.capabilities).filter(capability => plugins.capabilities().includes(capability.name) && ['provider', 'fallback'].includes(capability.role ?? 'provider'));
  const advertised = () => pluginContracts().filter(capability => !capability.name.startsWith('knowledge.') ? true : options.exposeKnowledgeMcp !== false && compatible(capability.version, '^1.0.0'));
  const contracts = (): Capability[] => closed ? [] : [...core.capabilities.map(name => ({ name, version: '1.0.0' })), { name: 'platform.capabilities', version: '1.0.0' }, { name: 'platform.invoke', version: '1.0.0' }, ...pluginContracts()];
  return {
    deviceId: core.deviceId, stateRoot: core.stateRoot, runtimeInstanceId: core.runtimeInstanceId, plugins,
    get capabilities() { return closed ? [] : [...new Set([...core.capabilities, 'platform.capabilities', 'platform.invoke', ...advertised().map(capability => capability.name)])]; },
    get capabilityContracts() { return contracts().filter(capability => options.exposeKnowledgeMcp !== false || !capability.name.startsWith('knowledge.')); },
    onCapabilitiesChanged: listener => plugins.onCapabilitiesChanged(listener),
    async invoke(tool, args = {}) {
      if (closed) throw Object.assign(new Error('Device runtime is closed'), { code: 'DEVICE_NOT_READY' });
      if (tool === 'platform.capabilities') return { deviceId: core.deviceId, contracts: contracts().filter(capability => options.exposeKnowledgeMcp !== false || !capability.name.startsWith('knowledge.')) };
      if (tool === 'platform.invoke') {
        const request = args as { name?: unknown; version?: unknown; input?: unknown };
        if (typeof request.name !== 'string' || request.name.length > 96 || (request.version !== undefined && (typeof request.version !== 'string' || request.version.length > 64))) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'A capability name and optional compatible contract version are required');
        return plugins.invoke(request.name, request.input ?? {}, { version: request.version as string | undefined });
      }
      if (plugins.capabilities().includes(tool)) return plugins.invoke(tool, args, { version: tool.startsWith('knowledge.') ? '^1.0.0' : undefined });
      try { const result = await core.invoke(tool, args); activity.record(tool, args, result); return result; }
      catch (error) { activity.record(tool, args, undefined, error); throw error; }
    },
    async invokeNative(tool, args = {}) {
      try { const result = await core.invokeNative(tool, args); activity.record(tool, args, result); return result; }
      catch (error) { activity.record(tool, args, undefined, error); throw error; }
    },
    close() {
      closed = true;
      if (!closeRequest) closeRequest = (async () => {
        const failures: unknown[] = [];
        for (const close of [() => plugins.close(), () => activity.close(), () => core.close()]) {
          try { await close(); } catch (error) { failures.push(error); }
        }
        if (failures.length) throw failures[0];
      })().catch(error => { closeRequest = undefined; throw error; });
      // A failed cleanup stays observable. Retrying performs real cleanup again;
      // disabling invocation alone is never evidence that resources were freed.
      return closeRequest;
    }
  };
}

/** Platform exchange contract. No knowledge provider package is imported here. */
export interface KnowledgeScope {
  projectId?: string;
  visibility: 'project' | 'account' | 'local';
}
export type KnowledgeStatus = 'hypothesis' | 'observed' | 'validated' | 'stale' | 'superseded' | 'withdrawn';
export interface KnowledgeEvidence {
  id: string;
  originDeviceId: string;
  sourceId: string;
  sourceRef: string;
  recordHash: string;
  kind: 'source_trace' | 'tool_receipt' | 'postcondition';
  sourceAvailable: boolean;
  observedAt: number;
}
export interface KnowledgeCard {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  contentHash: string;
  summary: string;
  body: string;
  status: KnowledgeStatus;
  originDeviceId: string;
  servingDeviceId: string;
  scope: KnowledgeScope;
  evidence: KnowledgeEvidence[];
  derivedFrom: string[];
  provenance: string[];
  updatedAt: number;
  lastVerifiedAt: number | null;
}
export interface KnowledgePreview {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  contentHash: string;
  summary: string;
  status: KnowledgeStatus;
  originDeviceId: string;
  servingDeviceId: string;
  scope: KnowledgeScope;
  read_ref: string;
  evidenceCount: number;
  lastVerifiedAt: number | null;
}
export const KNOWLEDGE_CONTRACT_VERSION = '1.0.0';

===== BEGIN SOURCE src/platform/knowledge-mcp.ts | original-bytes=8078 | sha256=3b143ba585327b448b831484b1efe685cced8d2e05b082942aa2de5b160bd5a4 =====
import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'node:async_hooks';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { KnowledgeRouter, unwrapKnowledgeResult, type KnowledgeDevice, type KnowledgeRequestContext } from './knowledge-router.js';
import { CapabilityError } from './plugin-contract.js';

interface BrokerDevice { deviceId: string; online: boolean; capabilities: string[]; projects?: string[]; lastSeenAt?: number; latencyMs?: number; }
export interface TrustedToolContext {
  accountId: string;
  sessionId: string;
  listDevices(): BrokerDevice[] | Promise<BrokerDevice[]>;
  callDevice(options: { deviceId: string; tool: string; arguments: Record<string, unknown>; timeoutMs?: number }): Promise<unknown>;
}
export interface PlatformTool {
  name: string;
  description: string;
  inputSchema: Record<string, unknown>;
  annotations: Record<string, boolean>;
  handler(args: Record<string, any>, context: TrustedToolContext): Promise<unknown>;
}

/** The key is runtime state, so references survive restart and verified restore. */
async function referenceKey(stateRoot: string): Promise<Buffer> {
  await fs.mkdir(stateRoot, { recursive: true });
  const filename = path.join(stateRoot, 'knowledge-reference.key');
  try { await fs.writeFile(filename, randomBytes(32), { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 }); }
  catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'EEXIST') throw error; }
  const key = await fs.readFile(filename);
  if (key.length !== 32) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_KEY_INVALID', 'Persisted knowledge reference key is invalid; restore the original key');
  return key;
}

/** All owner and caller policy checks still flow through the authenticated wrapper. */
export async function createPlatformTools(stateRoot: string): Promise<PlatformTool[]> {
  const requests = new AsyncLocalStorage<TrustedToolContext>();
  const trusted = (accountId: string) => {
    const context = requests.getStore();
    if (!context || !context.accountId || context.accountId !== accountId) throw new CapabilityError('AUTH_REQUIRED', 'An authenticated account context is required');
    return context;
  };
  const router = new KnowledgeRouter({
    async devices(accountId): Promise<KnowledgeDevice[]> {
      return (await trusted(accountId).listDevices()).map(device => ({ ...device, accountId }));
    },
    call: (accountId, deviceId, capability, input) => trusted(accountId).callDevice({ deviceId, tool: capability, arguments: input as Record<string, unknown>, timeoutMs: 30_000 })
  }, { secret: await referenceKey(stateRoot) });
  const properties = {
    device_id: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 64, description: 'Owned Device requesting the knowledge. It may have no knowledge plugin.' },
    project_id: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 512 },
    max_bytes: { type: 'integer', minimum: 1024, maximum: 65536, default: 16384 }
  };
  const contextFor = (args: Record<string, any>, context: TrustedToolContext): KnowledgeRequestContext => {
    if (!context.accountId || !context.sessionId) throw new CapabilityError('AUTH_REQUIRED', 'An authenticated account and session are required');
    return { accountId: context.accountId, sessionId: context.sessionId, requesterDeviceId: args.device_id };
  };
  const tools: PlatformTool[] = (['search', 'list'] as const).map(action => ({
    name: 'knowledge.' + action,
    description: action === 'search' ? 'Search compact knowledge previews across an account’s relevant available Devices. Does not import knowledge.' : 'List scoped knowledge previews on relevant available Devices. Does not import knowledge.',
    inputSchema: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, required: action === 'search' ? ['device_id', 'query'] : ['device_id'], properties: { ...properties, query: { type: 'string', maxLength: 4096 }, limit: { type: 'integer', minimum: 1, maximum: 50, default: 10 }, provider_device_id: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 64 } } },
    annotations: { readOnlyHint: true, destructiveHint: false, idempotentHint: true },
    handler: (args, context) => requests.run(context, () => router.search(contextFor(args, context), { query: args.query, projectId: args.project_id, limit: args.limit, maxBytes: args.max_bytes, providerDeviceId: args.provider_device_id }, action))
  }));
  tools.push({
    name: 'knowledge.read',
    description: 'Read one selected knowledge reference with provenance and freshness. Lazily import that item only when the requesting Device has a storage provider; otherwise use bounded session cache. Evidence is returned only when requested.',
    inputSchema: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, required: ['device_id', 'ref'], properties: { ...properties, ref: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 4096 }, evidence: { type: 'boolean', default: false } } },
    annotations: { readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: false, idempotentHint: true },
    handler: (args, context) => requests.run(context, () => router.read(contextFor(args, context), { ref: args.ref, projectId: args.project_id, evidence: args.evidence, maxBytes: args.max_bytes }))
  });
  tools.push({
    name: 'capability.list',
    description: 'Discover current capabilities advertised by an owned Device. Plugin disable, crash and dependency loss remove unavailable capabilities.',
    inputSchema: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, required: ['device_id'], properties: { device_id: properties.device_id } },
    annotations: { readOnlyHint: true, destructiveHint: false, idempotentHint: true },
    async handler(args, context) {
      if (!context.accountId) throw new CapabilityError('AUTH_REQUIRED', 'An authenticated account is required');
      const device = (await context.listDevices()).find(item => item.deviceId === args.device_id);
      if (!device) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Device is not owned by this account');
      const contracts = device.online && device.capabilities.includes('platform.capabilities') ? unwrapKnowledgeResult(await context.callDevice({ deviceId: device.deviceId, tool: 'platform.capabilities', arguments: {} })).contracts : undefined;
      return { device_id: device.deviceId, online: device.online, capabilities: device.capabilities, ...(contracts ? { contracts } : {}) };
    }
  }, {
    name: 'capability.call',
    description: 'Invoke an advertised plugin capability on an owned Device through normal caller policy. Knowledge exchange uses knowledge.search/list/read; native tools use their existing contracts.',
    inputSchema: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: false, required: ['device_id', 'capability', 'arguments'], properties: { device_id: properties.device_id, capability: { type: 'string', pattern: '^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*\\.[a-z0-9_.-]+$', maxLength: 96 }, version: { type: 'string', maxLength: 64, description: 'Required compatible capability contract, such as ^1.0.0 or 1.x.' }, arguments: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: true } } },
    annotations: { readOnlyHint: false, destructiveHint: false, idempotentHint: false },
    async handler(args, context) {
      if (!context.accountId) throw new CapabilityError('AUTH_REQUIRED', 'An authenticated account is required');
      if (String(args.capability).startsWith('knowledge.')) throw new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_PRIVATE', 'Use the public knowledge exchange contract');
      const device = (await context.listDevices()).find(item => item.deviceId === args.device_id);
      if (!device) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Device is not owned by this account');
      if (!device.online || !device.capabilities.includes('platform.invoke') || !device.capabilities.includes(args.capability)) throw new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE', 'The Device does not advertise this plugin capability');
      return context.callDevice({ deviceId: args.device_id, tool: 'platform.invoke', arguments: { name: args.capability, version: args.version, input: args.arguments }, timeoutMs: 30_000 });
    }
  });
  return tools;
}

===== BEGIN SOURCE src/platform/knowledge-router.ts | original-bytes=19521 | sha256=c9d053c5a2466a59d6f6b561a68c8d4dc7a03bd1ad5ae39b9494690f81d7b698 =====
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { CapabilityError } from './plugin-contract.js';
import type { KnowledgeCard, KnowledgePreview } from './knowledge-contract.js';

export interface KnowledgeDevice {
  deviceId: string;
  accountId: string;
  online: boolean;
  capabilities: string[];
  projects?: string[];
  lastSeenAt?: number;
  latencyMs?: number;
}
export interface KnowledgeTransport {
  devices(accountId: string): Promise<KnowledgeDevice[]>;
  call(accountId: string, deviceId: string, capability: string, input: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
}
export interface KnowledgeRequestContext { accountId: string; requesterDeviceId: string; sessionId: string; }
interface ReadReference { v: 1; account: string; provider: string; origin: string; id: string; version: number; project?: string; }
interface CacheEntry { card: KnowledgeCard; at: number; bytes: number; }
interface RevisionGuard { version: number; contentHash: string; status: KnowledgeCard['status']; at: number; blocked?: string; }
export function unwrapKnowledgeResult(value: any): any {
  if (value?.structuredContent !== undefined) return value.structuredContent;
  if (Array.isArray(value?.content)) {
    const text = value.content.find((item: any) => item.type === 'text')?.text;
    if (text) { try { return JSON.parse(text); } catch { /* domain result stays intact */ } }
  }
  return value;
}

/** Account-aware routing and lazy transfer live in the platform, not in a plugin. */
export class KnowledgeRouter {
  private cache = new Map<string, CacheEntry>();
  private cacheBytes = 0;
  // Session-bounded revision metadata prevents a known correction from falling back to old bytes.
  private revisions = new Map<string, RevisionGuard>();
  private readonly secret: Buffer;
  constructor(private transport: KnowledgeTransport, private options: { secret?: Buffer; cacheBytes?: number; cacheTtlMs?: number; maxProviders?: number; now?: () => number } = {}) {
    this.secret = options.secret ?? randomBytes(32);
    if (this.secret.length < 32) throw new Error('Knowledge reference key must be at least 256 bits');
  }
  private now(): number { return this.options.now?.() ?? Date.now(); }
  private accountTag(accountId: string): string { return createHmac('sha256', this.secret).update('account:' + accountId).digest('base64url'); }
  private reference(context: KnowledgeRequestContext, provider: string, card: Pick<KnowledgeCard, 'id' | 'originDeviceId' | 'version' | 'scope'>): string {
    const ref: ReadReference = { v: 1, account: this.accountTag(context.accountId), provider, origin: card.originDeviceId, id: card.id, version: card.version, project: card.scope.projectId };
    const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(ref)).toString('base64url');
    return 'k1.' + payload + '.' + createHmac('sha256', this.secret).update(payload).digest('base64url');
  }
  private parseReference(context: KnowledgeRequestContext, input: string): ReadReference {
    if (typeof input !== 'string' || input.length > 4096) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REF_INVALID', 'Invalid knowledge reference');
    const [prefix, payload, signature, extra] = input.split('.');
    if (prefix !== 'k1' || !payload || !signature || extra !== undefined) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REF_INVALID', 'Invalid knowledge reference');
    const expected = createHmac('sha256', this.secret).update(payload).digest();
    let received: Buffer; try { received = Buffer.from(signature, 'base64url'); } catch { throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REF_INVALID', 'Invalid reference signature'); }
    if (received.length !== expected.length || !timingSafeEqual(expected, received)) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REF_INVALID', 'Invalid reference signature');
    let ref: ReadReference; try { ref = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(payload, 'base64url').toString()); } catch { throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REF_INVALID', 'Invalid reference payload'); }
    if (ref.v !== 1 || ref.account !== this.accountTag(context.accountId)) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Knowledge reference belongs to another account');
    if (![ref.provider, ref.origin, ref.id].every(value => typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 0 && value.length <= 1024) || !Number.isSafeInteger(ref.version)) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REF_INVALID', 'Invalid reference fields');
    return ref;
  }
  private async scope(context: KnowledgeRequestContext): Promise<KnowledgeDevice[]> {
    if (!context.accountId || !context.requesterDeviceId || !context.sessionId) throw new CapabilityError('AUTH_REQUIRED', 'Authenticated account, requesting Device and session are required');
    const devices = (await this.transport.devices(context.accountId)).filter(device => device.accountId === context.accountId);
    if (!devices.some(device => device.deviceId === context.requesterDeviceId)) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Requesting Device is not owned by this account');
    return devices;
  }
  private allowed(card: Pick<KnowledgeCard, 'scope'>, providerId: string, context: KnowledgeRequestContext, projectId?: string): boolean {
    if (!card.scope || !['project', 'account', 'local'].includes(card.scope.visibility)) return false;
    if (card.scope.visibility === 'local' && providerId !== context.requesterDeviceId) return false;
    if (card.scope.projectId && card.scope.projectId !== projectId) return false;
    if (card.scope.visibility === 'project' && !card.scope.projectId) return false;
    return true;
  }
  private cacheKey(context: KnowledgeRequestContext, origin: string, id: string): string { return JSON.stringify([context.accountId, context.requesterDeviceId, context.sessionId, origin, id]); }
  private pruneCache(): void {
    for (const [key, entry] of this.cache) if (this.now() - entry.at > (this.options.cacheTtlMs ?? 300_000)) { this.cache.delete(key); this.cacheBytes -= entry.bytes; }
    for (const [key, entry] of this.revisions) if (this.now() - entry.at > (this.options.cacheTtlMs ?? 300_000)) this.revisions.delete(key);
    while (this.revisions.size > 256) this.revisions.delete(this.revisions.keys().next().value!);
    while (this.cache.size > 256 || this.cacheBytes > (this.options.cacheBytes ?? 8 * 1024 * 1024)) { const first = this.cache.entries().next().value; if (!first) break; this.cache.delete(first[0]); this.cacheBytes -= first[1].bytes; }
  }
  private forgetCached(key: string): void {
    const cached = this.cache.get(key);
    if (cached) { this.cache.delete(key); this.cacheBytes -= cached.bytes; }
  }
  private rememberRevision(key: string, card: Pick<KnowledgeCard, 'version' | 'contentHash' | 'status'>, blocked?: string): void {
    const prior = this.revisions.get(key);
    if (prior && (card.version < prior.version || (card.version === prior.version && (card.contentHash !== prior.contentHash || card.status !== prior.status)))) {
      throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REVISION_CONFLICT', 'Origin revision conflicts with previously observed knowledge');
    }
    this.revisions.delete(key);
    this.revisions.set(key, { version: card.version, contentHash: card.contentHash, status: card.status, at: this.now(), ...(blocked ? { blocked } : {}) });
    const cached = this.cache.get(key)?.card;
    if (blocked || (cached && (cached.version < card.version || (cached.version === card.version && (cached.contentHash !== card.contentHash || cached.status !== card.status))))) this.forgetCached(key);
    this.pruneCache();
  }
  private cacheCard(context: KnowledgeRequestContext, card: KnowledgeCard): void {
    const key = this.cacheKey(context, card.originDeviceId, card.id), bytes = Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(card));
    if (bytes > Math.min(1024 * 1024, this.options.cacheBytes ?? 8 * 1024 * 1024)) return;
    const previous = this.cache.get(key); if (previous) this.cacheBytes -= previous.bytes;
    this.cache.delete(key); this.cache.set(key, { card: structuredClone(card), at: this.now(), bytes }); this.cacheBytes += bytes; this.pruneCache();
  }
  cacheStats(): { entries: number; bytes: number } { this.pruneCache(); return { entries: this.cache.size, bytes: this.cacheBytes }; }
  private preview(value: any, context: KnowledgeRequestContext, provider: string): KnowledgePreview | undefined {
    if (!value || typeof value.id !== 'string' || typeof value.summary !== 'string' || typeof value.originDeviceId !== 'string' || !Number.isSafeInteger(value.version)) return;
    return {
      id: value.id, version: value.version, contentHash: String(value.contentHash ?? ''), summary: value.summary.slice(0, 1200), status: value.status,
      originDeviceId: value.originDeviceId, servingDeviceId: provider, scope: value.scope, evidenceCount: Number(value.evidenceCount ?? 0), lastVerifiedAt: value.lastVerifiedAt ?? null,
      read_ref: this.reference(context, provider, value)
    };
  }
  async search(context: KnowledgeRequestContext, input: { query?: string; projectId?: string; limit?: number; maxBytes?: number; providerDeviceId?: string }, action: 'search' | 'list' = 'search'): Promise<unknown> {
    const devices = await this.scope(context), capability = 'knowledge.' + action;
    const limit = Math.min(50, Math.max(1, input.limit ?? 10)), budget = Math.min(64 * 1024, Math.max(1024, input.maxBytes ?? 16 * 1024));
    const score = (device: KnowledgeDevice) => (device.deviceId === context.requesterDeviceId ? 1000 : 0) + (input.projectId && device.projects?.includes(input.projectId) ? 500 : 0) - Math.min(device.latencyMs ?? 100, 500) / 10;
    const providers = devices.filter(device => device.online && device.capabilities.includes(capability) && (!input.providerDeviceId || input.providerDeviceId === device.deviceId)).sort((a, b) => score(b) - score(a) || (b.lastSeenAt ?? 0) - (a.lastSeenAt ?? 0) || a.deviceId.localeCompare(b.deviceId));
    if (input.providerDeviceId && !devices.some(d => d.deviceId === input.providerDeviceId)) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Provider Device is not owned by this account');
    const items: KnowledgePreview[] = [], queried: string[] = [], unavailable: { deviceId: string; code: string }[] = [], dedup = new Set<string>();
    let truncated = false;
    // A relevant successful provider is enough; fallback is bounded and only used for absence/failure.
    for (const provider of providers.slice(0, Math.min(3, Math.max(1, this.options.maxProviders ?? 2)))) {
      queried.push(provider.deviceId);
      try {
        const result = unwrapKnowledgeResult(await this.transport.call(context.accountId, provider.deviceId, capability, { query: input.query, projectId: input.projectId, limit, maxBytes: budget }));
        for (const candidate of result.items ?? []) {
          if (!this.allowed(candidate, provider.deviceId, context, input.projectId) || ['withdrawn', 'superseded'].includes(candidate.status)) continue;
          const item = this.preview(candidate, context, provider.deviceId); if (!item) continue;
          const key = item.originDeviceId + ':' + item.id; if (dedup.has(key)) continue;
          if (items.length >= limit || Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify({ items: [...items, item], queried, unavailable, truncated: true })) > budget) { truncated = true; break; }
          dedup.add(key); items.push(item);
        }
        truncated ||= Boolean(result.truncated);
        if (items.length) break;
      } catch (error) { unavailable.push({ deviceId: provider.deviceId, code: (error as CapabilityError).code ?? 'PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE' }); }
    }
    const response = { items, queried, unavailable, truncated, imported: 0 };
    while (Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response)) > budget && response.items.length) { response.items.pop(); response.truncated = true; }
    if (Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response)) > budget) throw new CapabilityError('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL', 'Routing metadata exceeds the response budget');
    return response;
  }
  async read(context: KnowledgeRequestContext, input: { ref: string; projectId?: string; evidence?: boolean; maxBytes?: number }): Promise<unknown> {
    const devices = await this.scope(context), ref = this.parseReference(context, input.ref);
    if (input.projectId !== undefined && ref.project !== undefined && input.projectId !== ref.project) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Reference does not belong to the requested project');
    const projectId = input.projectId ?? ref.project;
    const provider = devices.find(device => device.deviceId === ref.provider), requester = devices.find(device => device.deviceId === context.requesterDeviceId)!;
    if (!provider) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Serving Device is no longer owned by this account');
    const key = this.cacheKey(context, ref.origin, ref.id);
    this.pruneCache();
    const cached = this.cache.get(key);
    let card: KnowledgeCard | undefined, evidence: unknown;
    let freshness: 'current' | 'stale' = 'current';
    const assertRevision = (value: Pick<KnowledgeCard, 'version' | 'contentHash' | 'status'>): void => {
      if (!value || !Number.isSafeInteger(value.version) || value.version < 1 || typeof value.contentHash !== 'string' || !value.contentHash || value.contentHash.length > 128 ||
          !['hypothesis', 'observed', 'validated', 'stale', 'superseded', 'withdrawn'].includes(value.status)) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REVISION_INVALID', 'Provider returned invalid revision metadata');
      const known = this.revisions.get(key), minimum = Math.max(ref.version, known?.version ?? 0);
      if (value.version < minimum) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REVISION_UNAVAILABLE', 'The selected or newer known knowledge revision is unavailable');
      if (known && value.version === known.version && (value.contentHash !== known.contentHash || value.status !== known.status)) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REVISION_CONFLICT', 'Provider returned conflicting bytes for a known revision');
    };
    const fetchCard = async (device: KnowledgeDevice): Promise<void> => {
      const result = unwrapKnowledgeResult(await this.transport.call(context.accountId, device.deviceId, 'knowledge.read', { ref: ref.id, projectId, evidence: input.evidence === true, maxBytes: Math.min(64 * 1024, Math.max(1024, input.maxBytes ?? 16 * 1024)) }));
      if (!result.card || result.card.id !== ref.id || result.card.originDeviceId !== ref.origin) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH', 'Provider returned a different knowledge identity');
      assertRevision(result.card);
      if (!this.allowed(result.card, device.deviceId, context, projectId)) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Knowledge scope does not permit this read');
      // Routing identity comes from the authenticated transport, never from provider-supplied metadata.
      card = { ...result.card, servingDeviceId: device.deviceId }; evidence = result.evidence;
    };
    const useCache = (): boolean => {
      if (!cached || this.revisions.get(key)?.blocked) return false;
      try { assertRevision(cached.card); } catch { return false; }
      card = structuredClone(cached.card); freshness = 'stale'; return true;
    };
    // Prefer a previously imported copy on the requesting Device, if it has one.
    if (requester.online && requester.deviceId !== provider.deviceId && requester.capabilities.includes('knowledge.read')) {
      try { await fetchCard(requester); } catch { /* selected provider remains authoritative fallback */ }
    }
    if (!card && !input.evidence) useCache();
    if (!card && provider.online && provider.capabilities.includes('knowledge.read')) await fetchCard(provider);
    if (!card) useCache();
    if (!card) {
      const blocked = this.revisions.get(key)?.blocked;
      if (blocked) throw new CapabilityError(blocked, 'Origin denied or removed this knowledge');
      if (this.revisions.has(key)) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REVISION_UNAVAILABLE', 'The newer known knowledge revision is unavailable');
      throw new CapabilityError('PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE', 'Knowledge provider is offline and this session has no cached selected read');
    }
    const origin = devices.find(device => device.deviceId === card!.originDeviceId);
    if (origin?.online && origin.capabilities.includes('knowledge.stat')) {
      let metadata: any, metadataReceived = false;
      try {
        metadata = unwrapKnowledgeResult(await this.transport.call(context.accountId, origin.deviceId, 'knowledge.stat', { ref: card.id, projectId })); metadataReceived = true;
      } catch (error) {
        const code = (error as CapabilityError).code;
        if (code === 'ACCESS_DENIED' || code === 'KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND' || code === 'KNOWLEDGE_SCOPE_MISMATCH') {
          this.rememberRevision(key, this.revisions.get(key) ?? card, code); throw error;
        }
        freshness = 'stale';
      }
      if (metadataReceived) {
        if (!metadata || typeof metadata !== 'object') throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_REVISION_INVALID', 'Origin returned invalid revision metadata');
        if ((metadata.id !== undefined && metadata.id !== ref.id) || (metadata.originDeviceId !== undefined && metadata.originDeviceId !== ref.origin)) throw new CapabilityError('KNOWLEDGE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH', 'Origin returned different revision metadata');
        assertRevision(metadata);
        const wasBlocked = this.revisions.get(key)?.blocked;
        this.rememberRevision(key, metadata, wasBlocked);
        // Once origin confirms change, failure to fetch it must never resurrect/import older bytes.
        if (wasBlocked || metadata.version !== card.version || metadata.contentHash !== card.contentHash || metadata.status !== card.status) {
          try { await fetchCard(origin); }
          catch (error) {
            const code = (error as CapabilityError).code;
            if (code === 'ACCESS_DENIED' || code === 'KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND' || code === 'KNOWLEDGE_SCOPE_MISMATCH') this.rememberRevision(key, metadata, code);
            throw error;
          }
        }
        assertRevision(card);
        this.rememberRevision(key, card);
        freshness = 'current';
      }
    } else if (!origin?.online || card.servingDeviceId !== card.originDeviceId) freshness = 'stale';
    const blocked = this.revisions.get(key)?.blocked;
    if (blocked) throw new CapabilityError(blocked, 'Origin denied or removed this knowledge');
    assertRevision(card);
    if (!this.allowed(card, card.servingDeviceId, context, projectId)) throw new CapabilityError('ACCESS_DENIED', 'Knowledge scope does not permit this read');
    const response = { card, ...(evidence === undefined ? {} : { evidence }), imported: false, freshness: { status: freshness, lastVerifiedAt: freshness === 'current' ? this.now() : card.lastVerifiedAt }, read_ref: this.reference(context, card.servingDeviceId, card) };
    if (Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response)) > Math.min(64 * 1024, Math.max(1024, input.maxBytes ?? 16 * 1024))) throw new CapabilityError('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL', 'Full selected knowledge and provenance exceed the response budget');
    if (requester.online && requester.capabilities.includes('knowledge.import') && card.originDeviceId !== requester.deviceId) {
      const result = unwrapKnowledgeResult(await this.transport.call(context.accountId, requester.deviceId, 'knowledge.import', { card })); response.imported = Boolean(result.imported);
    }
    this.rememberRevision(key, card);
    this.cacheCard(context, card);
    return response;
  }
}

import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import readline from 'node:readline';
import { CapabilityError, type CapabilityRequirement, type PluginContext, type PluginInstance, type PluginModule } from './plugin-contract.js';
import { createPluginServices } from './plugin-services.js';

// Generic JSON-lines adapter; non-Node sidecars implement the same request/callback frames.
let instance: PluginInstance | undefined;
let initializing = false;
let requirements: CapabilityRequirement[] = [];
const controllers = new Map<string, AbortController>();
const hostPending = new Map<string, { resolve(value: unknown): void; reject(error: Error): void; cleanup(): void }>();
const send = (value: unknown) => process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(value) + '\n');
console.log = (...args: unknown[]) => console.error(...args);
const servicesFor = (parentId: string, signal: AbortSignal) => createPluginServices(requirements, (name, input, options) => {
  const id = randomUUID();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const abort = () => {
      if (!hostPending.delete(id)) return;
      send({ type: 'host_cancel', id, parent_id: parentId });
      reject(new CapabilityError('CANCELLED', 'Dependency callback cancelled'));
    };
    options.signal.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true });
    hostPending.set(id, { resolve, reject, cleanup: () => options.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abort) });
    send({ type: 'host_call', id, parent_id: parentId, method: 'capability.invoke', params: { name, input } });
  });
}, signal);
const lines = readline.createInterface({ input: process.stdin, crlfDelay: Infinity });
lines.on('line', async (line) => {
  let request: { id: string; method: string; type?: string; params?: any; result?: unknown; error?: { code?: string; message?: string } };
  try {
    if (Buffer.byteLength(line) > 2 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('Plugin request exceeds frame budget');
    request = JSON.parse(line);
    if (!request || typeof request.id !== 'string') throw new Error('Invalid request id');
    if (request.type === 'host_result') {
      const pending = hostPending.get(request.id);
      if (pending) {
        hostPending.delete(request.id); pending.cleanup();
        if (request.error) pending.reject(new CapabilityError(request.error.code ?? 'CAPABILITY_CALL_FAILED', request.error.message ?? 'Dependency failed'));
        else pending.resolve(request.result);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (request.type !== undefined || typeof request.method !== 'string') throw new Error('Invalid request frame');
  } catch (error) { send({ id: null, error: { code: 'PLUGIN_PROTOCOL_ERROR', message: String(error) } }); return; }
  const { id, method, params } = request;
  const controller = new AbortController();
  if (controllers.has(id)) { send({ id, error: { code: 'PLUGIN_PROTOCOL_ERROR', message: 'Duplicate request id' } }); return; }
  controllers.set(id, controller);
  const invocation = { signal: controller.signal, services: servicesFor(id, controller.signal) };
  try {
    if (method === 'init') {
      if (instance || initializing) throw new Error('Plugin already initialized');
      initializing = true; requirements = params.requires ?? [];
      const mod = await import(pathToFileURL(params.entry).href) as PluginModule;
      const context = { ...params.context, services: servicesFor(id, controller.signal) } as PluginContext;
      if (context.previousSchemaVersion !== undefined && context.previousSchemaVersion !== params.schemaVersion) {
        if (!mod.migrate) throw new Error('Plugin data schema changed without a migration hook');
        await mod.migrate({ dataRoot: context.dataRoot, fromVersion: context.previousSchemaVersion, toVersion: params.schemaVersion });
      }
      instance = await mod.createPlugin(context);
      if (!instance || typeof instance.invoke !== 'function') throw new Error('Plugin does not implement invoke');
      await instance.start?.({ signal: controller.signal, services: servicesFor(id, controller.signal) });
      send({ id, result: { ready: true } });
    } else if (method === 'invoke') {
      if (!instance) throw new Error('Plugin not initialized');
      send({ id, result: await instance.invoke(params.capability, params.input, invocation) });
    } else if (method === 'cancel') {
      controllers.get(params.id)?.abort(); send({ id, result: { cancelled: true } });
    } else if (method === 'stop') {
      for (const [otherId, other] of controllers) if (otherId !== id) other.abort();
      await instance?.stop?.(invocation); instance = undefined;
      send({ id, result: { stopped: true } });
      lines.close(); process.stdin.destroy();
    } else throw new Error('Unknown plugin protocol method');
  } catch (error) {
    const e = error as Error & { code?: string };
    send({ id, error: { code: e.code ?? 'PLUGIN_CALL_FAILED', message: e.message ?? String(error) } });
  } finally {
    controller.abort(); controllers.delete(id);
    if (method === 'init') initializing = false;
  }
});

import type { PluginServices } from './plugin-services.js';

export type CapabilityRole = 'provider' | 'augmenter' | 'observer' | 'fallback';
export interface Capability {
  name: string;
  version: string;
  role?: CapabilityRole;
  priority?: number;
}
export interface CapabilityRequirement { name: string; version: string; }
export interface PluginManifest {
  id: string;
  version: string;
  dataSchemaVersion: number;
  capabilities: Capability[];
  requires?: CapabilityRequirement[];
  isolation: 'process' | 'in-process';
  /** In-process code is trusted and must not block the JS event loop. */
  trusted?: boolean;
  entry: string;
  /** Optional language-independent JSON-lines executable. */
  executable?: string;
  args?: string[];
  timeoutMs?: number;
}
export interface PluginContext {
  deviceId: string;
  dataRoot: string;
  projectRoots: string[];
  sourceRoots: string[];
  previousSchemaVersion?: number;
  settings?: Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Init-scoped dependency services; invoke/start receive a fresh scope. */
  services?: PluginServices;
}
export interface InvocationContext { signal: AbortSignal; services: PluginServices; }
export interface PluginInstance {
  start?(context?: InvocationContext): Promise<void> | void;
  stop?(context?: InvocationContext): Promise<void> | void;
  invoke(capability: string, input: unknown, context: InvocationContext): Promise<unknown> | unknown;
}
export interface PluginModule {
  createPlugin(context: PluginContext): Promise<PluginInstance> | PluginInstance;
  migrate?(options: { dataRoot: string; fromVersion: number; toVersion: number }): Promise<void> | void;
}
export type PluginState = 'installed' | 'enabled' | 'disabled' | 'faulted' | 'uninstalled' | 'missing';
export interface PluginRecord {
  manifest: PluginManifest;
  enabled: boolean;
  installed: boolean;
  schemaVersion: number;
  state: PluginState;
  error?: string;
  /** Only ensureBundled creates distribution provenance. Unmarked records are user-managed. */
  installationSource?: 'distribution' | 'manual';
  /** Persisted gate: replaced distribution bytes must not run under stale metadata. */
  bundleUpgradeRequired?: { version: string; dataSchemaVersion: number };
}
export class CapabilityError extends Error {
  constructor(public readonly code: string, message: string) { super(message); this.name = 'CapabilityError'; }
}
export function compatible(version: string, range: string): boolean {
  const actual = version.match(/^(\d+)\.(\d+)\.(\d+)$/);
  if (!actual) return false;
  if (range === '*' || range === version) return true;
  const requested = range.match(/^(?:\^)?(\d+)(?:\.(\d+|x|\*))?(?:\.(\d+|x|\*))?$/);
  if (!requested || actual[1] !== requested[1]) return false;
  if (range.startsWith('^')) {
    if (!/^\^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(range)) return false;
    const minor = Number(requested[2] ?? 0), patch = Number(requested[3] ?? 0);
    if (Number(actual[1]) === 0) return Number(actual[2]) === minor && (minor === 0 ? Number(actual[3]) === patch : Number(actual[3]) >= patch);
    return Number(actual[2]) > minor || (Number(actual[2]) === minor && Number(actual[3]) >= patch);
  }
  return (!requested[2] || /[x*]/.test(requested[2]) || requested[2] === actual[2]) &&
    (!requested[3] || /[x*]/.test(requested[3]) || requested[3] === actual[3]);
}
export function validateManifest(value: PluginManifest): void {
  if (!value || !/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$/.test(value.id)) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MANIFEST_INVALID', 'Invalid plugin id');
  if (!/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:[-+][\w.-]+)?$/.test(value.version)) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MANIFEST_INVALID', 'Invalid plugin version');
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value.dataSchemaVersion) || value.dataSchemaVersion < 1) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MANIFEST_INVALID', 'Invalid data schema');
  if (!['process', 'in-process'].includes(value.isolation) || (value.isolation === 'in-process' && value.trusted !== true)) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_ISOLATION_REQUIRED', 'In-process plugins must explicitly declare cooperative trusted execution');
  if (typeof value.entry !== 'string' || !value.entry || !Array.isArray(value.capabilities) || value.capabilities.length > 128) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MANIFEST_INVALID', 'Invalid entry/capabilities');
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const c of value.capabilities) {
    const key = `${c.name}:${c.role ?? 'provider'}`;
    if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{0,95}$/.test(c.name) || !/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(c.version) || seen.has(key) || !['provider', 'augmenter', 'observer', 'fallback'].includes(c.role ?? 'provider')) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MANIFEST_INVALID', 'Invalid or duplicate capability');
    if (c.priority !== undefined && (!Number.isSafeInteger(c.priority) || Math.abs(c.priority) > 10000)) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MANIFEST_INVALID', 'Invalid capability priority');
    seen.add(key);
  }
  if (new Set((value.requires ?? []).map(requirement => requirement.name)).size !== (value.requires ?? []).length) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MANIFEST_INVALID', 'Duplicate capability requirement');
  for (const r of value.requires ?? []) if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9_.-]{0,95}$/.test(r.name) || typeof r.version !== 'string' || !(r.version === '*' || /^\^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(r.version) || /^\d+(?:\.(?:\d+|x|\*)){0,2}$/.test(r.version))) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MANIFEST_INVALID', 'Invalid capability requirement or unsupported version range');
}
